HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_06a8b1ca479fa053f4b2","text":"1. **Ease as opened route and readying.** Evidence: `ي س ر` B001 (ease/readiness after hardship). Structural cue: the duplicated root in verb and noun makes the mechanism recursive. Mechanism: the one oriented by giving is not merely rewarded with ease; he is actively readied for the easy course. Abductive move: I infer that divine facilitation amplifies prior orientation. Reading change: ease is a process of being fitted to a path.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1ee5249d9b593494cd25","text":"92:11’s explicit failure of wealth corrected `ي س ر:B003`: genuine means cannot simply equal possessions. Then 92:12 made the repeated facilitation directional, revealing that the person is fitted to a course rather than merely granted a locally easy moment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_383a6a2ed63020f059a7","text":"3. **A trained gait away from collapse.** `ي س ر:B005` gives the concrete image of light, pliant movement. Purposeful striving in 92:4 and the fall of 92:11 turn that image into a spatial mechanism: facilitation makes one gait responsive, while the opposed course culminates in uncontrolled descent. The movement analogy and causal contrast are inferred from the supplied sequence. The ayah consequently reads as the shaping of how one moves, not simply the removal of obstacles.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_417b1910b9da9467b411","text":"2. **Ease as real means rather than claimed independence.** `ي س ر:B003` supplies prosperity and amplitude of means. Its activation comes from the nearby claim of self-sufficiency in 92:8 and the explicit failure of wealth to avail in 92:11. The structural cue is a lexical economy of means: one sequence receives `يُسْرَىٰ`, while the other claims `غِنَى` that later fails. The inferred correction is that usable sufficiency is conferred alignment, not mere possession. Ease can therefore denote genuine capacity to proceed, sharply distinguished from wealth-backed self-sufficiency.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B004","root_001694/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_444a1a060138f15ead2a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:7:1\",\"root_001694\",[\"B004\",\"B007\"]],[\"92:7:2\",\"root_001694\",[\"B004\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Sol taraf ve kura payları boyutları, kolay yolu alışılmış pay hesabının dışına çıkan bir hareket olarak düşündürebilir; bağ, ayetin açık düzenini açıklamak için fazla dolaylıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5a6164bfba05292d8e0a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:7:1\",\"root_001694\",[\"B005\"]],[\"92:7:2\",\"root_001694\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kolaylaştırma, düşüşün karşısında hafif ve yönlendirilebilir bir yürüyüş tarzı kazandırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6cc9ed2a15ab237d091e","text":"92:19-20 refined the motive: the same act of giving purifies only because no repayable favor controls it and because the Lord’s face is the aim. Mechanism: a route can be “easy” while also cutting across ordinary right/left or share-calculation schemas. Abductive move: I infer the easy path disrupts transactional lot-allocation. Reading change: facilitation is not winning a share by lots but being moved beyond the shares system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8c742e378e06d08f2c65","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:7:1\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:7:2\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Fiil ile hedefte tekrarlanan kolaylık, kişiyi yalnızca rahatlatmaz; onu ilerledikçe kolay yolun biçimine uyduran bir hazırlama süreci kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d536223061a41c2d229e","text":"2. **Prosperity redefined away from hoarding.** Evidence: `ي س ر` B003 (means/prosperity), activated by 92:8 `غ ن ي` self-sufficiency and 92:18-19 wealth without repayment.
